# Passive Label/Image Cover Overlap Audit (2026-11-28)
Generated from `python3 scripts/validate_synthyboy_skin.py --list-covers skin` (after merging `ng/oscdropdown`, commit `51cc4f0`).
- Total overlapping `(tab, touchable, cover, source)` pairs: **2116**
- Distinct `(cover_source, cover_name, cover_type)` groups: **92** (**87** REAL / 2101 pairs; **5** FALSE POSITIVE / 15 pairs)

## Needs a Plugin Gate / Structural Fix (cannot be fixed by gates or non-visual reorder in this task)
| Cover | Definition (`file:line`) | Overlapped Touchable (`file:line`) | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` (Label, `2 171 142 30`) | `defs_fx.json:fxNativeGalleryCard` (`skin/defs_fx.json:1975`) | `button` (`skin/defs_fx.json:1954`) | Opaque `fx_chain_gallery_card.png` (`alpha=255`); moving before `button` hides text. Needs `Button` -> `Image`+`actions` refactor (`oscNativeSetting_action` pattern). |
| `name` (Label, `0.025 0 0.95 1`) | `defs_lfo.json:lfoDetailRow` (`skin/defs_lfo.json:5897`) | ` ` (`skin/defs_lfo.json:5874`) | Opaque `native_action_e4feb6764923e3caf30d.png` (`alpha=255`); moving before `button` hides text. Needs `Button` -> `Image`+`actions` refactor. |
| `name` (Label, `0 0 1 1`) | `defs_lfo.json:lfoEditAction` (`skin/defs_lfo.json:6136`) | ` ` (`skin/defs_lfo.json:6114`) | Opaque `native_action_36f4ad4ed373ad966055.png` (`alpha=255`); moving before `button` hides text. Needs `Button` -> `Image`+`actions` refactor. |
| `Native Osc Message` (Label, `8 210 978 40`) | `TUI.json:tab:Osc A/B/C` (`skin/TUI.json:8848,9121,9394`) | `LOAD A/B/C` (`347 245 300 64`, `skin/defs_osc.json:19518-19627`) | 5 px vertical overlap (`y=245..250`) while both `"NO SAMPLE"` and `LOAD A/B/C` are shown simultaneously (`osc_display.h:262`, `osc_ui.h:2658`); moving behind `Screen` dims `"OFF"` under `off veil` (`alpha=184`). Needs `Native Osc Message` bounds trim (`h <= 34`). |

Note on `*Header*` definitions: `defs_mod.json:modHeader` (`Count`, `skin/defs_mod.json:3681`) and `defs_lfo.json:lfoHeader` (`Title`, `skin/defs_lfo.json:17504`) are generated by `scripts/header-skin.py` from `components/native/header/skin-{mod,lfo}.json` and are not hand-edited; their tab instances in `skin/TUI.json` (`Mod Header`, `LFO 1..10 Header`, `RND 1..4 Header`) receive the exact view gates.
